【聚焦當期景氣兌現，機構積極挖掘三季報投資線索】多位業內人士研判，整體而言，全A非金融行業三季度業績有望延續高增長態勢；結構上，科技與資源行業雙輪景氣驅動的格局將進一步強化，行業配置的核心思路也應由遠期空間轉向當期景氣兌現。在具體配置上，考慮到10年期美債收益率高企成為約束市場表現尤其是科技板塊反彈高度的重要因素，因此短期建議配置景氣品種和供給出清品種來應對高利率環境，10月配置圍繞科技成長、資源紅利、製造出海三條線索展開，兼顧進攻與防禦。（中證報）

【奇瑞汽車、比亞迪、上汽集團等都在抓緊研發，固態電池“上車”提速】多家中國車企的固態電池研發業務程序正在提速。10月12日，奇瑞汽車有關負責人確認，公司電池事業部的固態電池研發已在穩妥推進中。10月8日，奇瑞汽車集中釋出多項2026年固態電池裝置採購專案，涵蓋固態電池研發、製備、檢測等核心環節。不僅是奇瑞汽車，記者採訪獲悉，比亞迪、上汽集團、東風汽車等國內主流有新能源業務的車企集團近期都在抓緊固態電池的研發工作。（上證報）

【UKMTO: Six vessels attacked same day in Strait of Hormuz and nearby waters】UK Maritime Trade Operations issued multiple notices reporting six vessels were attacked on the 11th in the Strait of Hormuz and adjacent Gulf of Oman. In the Strait, four tankers were struck by "unidentified projectiles" while exiting and a container ship was hit while entering. Near the eastern entrance at Sohar anchorage in the Gulf of Oman, a tanker was struck in a drone attack; local authorities assisted crew evacuation. Since late September such attacks have continued, casualties have risen and incidents have spread into waters off Qatar, the UAE and Oman amid escalating US–Iran contestation over control of the strait.

【Consumer orders recover; China e-commerce logistics index edges up in September】China e-commerce logistics index rose to 111.9 in September, up 0.4 points MoM. ### 2026-10-13 04:40:16  #公司

【葉門胡塞武裝再襲沙特首都機場並打擊一處空軍基地】葉門胡塞武裝12日說，該組織當天向沙烏地阿拉伯首都利雅得的哈立德國王國際機場發射一枚彈道導彈，並使用無人機襲擊沙特西南部一處空軍基地。胡塞武裝發言人葉海亞·薩雷亞當天在社交媒體發表宣告說，該組織12日晚向利雅得哈立德國王國際機場發射的導彈命中目標；該組織還使用無人機襲擊了位於沙特西南部海米斯穆謝特的哈立德國王空軍基地，無人機也命中目標。薩雷亞再次警告國際航空公司、航空從業人員及旅客不要使用沙特境內的機場和領空，稱除麥加和麥地那的機場及空域外，沙特機場均“不安全”。近日，胡塞武裝連續襲擊沙特境內的機場和石油設施。沙特主導的多國聯軍則表示，正開展大規模軍事行動，打擊胡塞武裝人員及其軍事能力，著力瓦解其彈道導彈網路，並全天候為葉門政府軍提供空中支援。（新華社）

US Stocks End Mixed, With Tech Shares Under Pressure — The S&P 500 closed little changed and the Nasdaq fell 0.4%, while the Dow gained 196 points on Monday. Tech led the declines, while energy, communication services and consumer staples outperformed. Investors are preparing for the earnings season while awaiting key US inflation data due later this week. Meanwhile, oil prices rose following deadly strikes on Riyadh’s airport over the weekend. Prices also drew support from renewed concerns over energy infrastructure after Ukrainian President Zelenskyy said Russia had continued targeting energy facilities, despite US President Trump’s highlighting fresh Russian diesel exports and a Russia-Ukraine ceasefire on energy targets. Micron tumbled 3.9% as investors assessed growing competition from China’s CXMT and Nvidia fell 1.3%. In contrast, Meta gained 3.4% following the release of a long-awaited AI model, while Salesforce advanced 2.1% after Bank of America upgraded the stock. Chevron also rose 1.4% amid higher oil prices.

【美國9月二手房簽約量創近十二年最低 房貸利率飆升重挫購房需求】隨著房貸利率重返7%以上，美國房地產市場進一步降溫。上月賣家紛紛降價，為數不多的買家則掌握了更多議價權。房地產經紀公司Redfin經季節性調整的資料顯示，9月二手房簽約量環比大跌4%至2014年以來最低水平，降幅創逾三年來最大（2020年新冠疫情初期的幾個月除外）。與此同時，待售房屋供應量升至2020年3月以來最高水平。Redfin首席經濟學家Daryl Fairweather表示：“房地產行業的人氣明顯轉變。利率像這樣上升時，能夠負擔得起買房的人會減少，這意味著銷量必然下降。”房地美資料顯示，30年期房貸平均利率從8月底的6.66%躍升至10月8日的7.4%，創近三年來最高水平，並較上年同期高出逾1個百分點。

### 2026-10-13 01:47:13  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy #Brent #Impact bullish #WTI

【At least 10 VLCCs avoid Fujairah as risk of Iranian attacks near Strait of Hormuz rises】Ship-tracking data show at least 10 very large crude carriers (VLCCs) and several Suezmax tankers bound for Fujairah have paused or turned back as the perceived risk of Iranian attacks near the Strait of Hormuz rises. Fujairah is a major transshipment hub for Persian Gulf oil exports; VLCCs often wait outside the strait and transship via smaller tankers. Withdrawal of large tankers could disrupt that bypass pattern and add pressure to crude logistics. Some vessels remain en route, but repeated recent attacks near the Strait of Hormuz and Fujairah have raised market concern about the continuity of oil shipments.

They warned transport disruptions have driven shipping and insurance costs to unprecedented levels—exceeding one-third of related cargo value—hindering timely crude and refined-fuel shipments and adding to global inflationary pressure; high transport costs are expected to pass along the oil supply chain, raising costs for producers and consumers and weighing on growth. Ministers said they will keep monitoring the situation and work with governments, industry and international bodies including the IEF and IEA to improve global shipping conditions.

### 2026-10-13 00:57:46  #MKT News #Macro & Rates #The Fed (FOMC) #DXY #Impact bullish #US10Y #Impact bearish #S&P500

【Hammack: Fed needs more tightening to restore disinflation to 2%】Cleveland Fed Chair Hammack said policy must move to restrictive levels to drive disinflation back to the 2% target, warning the current stance risks a longer-than-acceptable path and therefore requires more tightening. She left open the possibility of further rate hikes but gave no timing, and said she favors small, more frequent adjustments to help firms and households plan. Hammack flagged rising input costs and increasing pass-through to consumers; if prices are no longer constrained by demand, inf could rise or remain elevated. The biggest risk is firms and households forming higher inf expectations, and she said there are early signs of that. On US yields, she cited stronger growth expectations, market repricing of Fed policy, fiscal pressures and heavy issuance; data-center investment is boosting growth but financial conditions still need further tightening. She reiterated that Fed independence remains crucial.

### 2026-10-13 00:41:07  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Lumber Rises to One-Month High — Lumber futures rose to $540 per thousand board feet, their highest level in nearly a month, as Hurricane Isaias threatened supplies across key producing regions. The storm swept through the US Gulf Coast, causing major power outages in Alabama and Mississippi, both significant lumber-producing states, raising concerns over supply disruptions and pressuring prices. Lumber supplies were already constrained by escalating trade tensions between the US and Canada. Ottawa had announced counter-tariffs of 25% on US lumber and 50% on plywood in response to US tariffs on several lumber-related products. US forestry groups have long accused Canada of distorting the market, arguing that the largely public owned Canadian forestland gives domestic producers a cost advantage. However, gains remain tempered by weakness in the US housing market. Mortgage rates have climbed to 7.4%, their highest in nearly three years, raising borrowing costs for homebuyers and weighing on housing demand.

### 2026-10-12 22:35:34  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Platinum Trades Near 10-Week Low — Platinum futures traded around $1,700 an ounce, remaining near its lowest level in ten weeks, as both near and longer term outlooks remain subdued. Tanker attacks in the Strait of Hormuz lifted oil prices and raised concerns over inflationary pressures, keeping traders cautious and providing some support. However, while markets widely expect the Federal Reserve to leave interest rates unchanged this month, the prospect of a rate hike by year-end could weigh on investment flows into the metal. Over the longer term, the WPIC sees total demand falling 18%, including a 32% drop in Chinese jewelry demand and a 4% decline in automotive demand, although resilient demand for hybrids has helped moderate the decline. Despite this, the drop in demand continues to underpin a bearish outlook for the metal as it outpaces the contraction in supply, with elevated operating costs and power grid instability in South Africa, alongside ongoing trade risks in Russia, constraining production.

### 2026-10-12 22:24:41  #央行 #宏觀 #公司 #市場 #國際

【哈薩克央行擬減持黃金 將黃金儲備佔比降低約3個百分點】據報道，哈薩克央行正從全球最大的黃金買家之一轉變為黃金賣家，財政部官員正加大力度推動儲備資產再平衡。近年來，各國央行大舉購金推動金價大幅上漲，但隨著黃金價格創下歷史新高後回落，這輪漲勢遭遇阻力。美國10年期國債收益率上週攀升至24年來最高水平。哈薩克央行副行長Aliya Moldabekova在採訪中表示：“我們很久沒有看到這樣的水平了。這使美元成為一個具有吸引力的選擇，從而降低了黃金作為投資資產的吸引力。”她補充稱，哈薩克央行希望將黃金佔外匯儲備總額的比例降低約3個百分點。多年來，各國央行一直在穩步增持黃金。但今年早些時候伊朗戰爭爆發後，貨幣機構的購金需求持續旺盛這一假設受到挑戰。這場戰爭引發了全球對美元的需求熱潮，以支付成本更高的能源進口。根據世界黃金協會的資料，土耳其和俄羅斯是今年較早減持黃金的國家之一。

### 2026-10-12 22:16:57  #Trading Economics #Economy #El Salvador #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

El Salvador PPI Slows in September — Producer prices in El Salvador rose 3.42% year-on-year in September 2026, easing from a 3.52% increase in August. The slowdown was largely driven by lower inflation in manufacturing (2.72% vs 3%), other service activities (2.73% vs 3.64%), electricity, gas, steam and air conditioning (23.1% vs 23.38%) and administrative and support services (1.65% vs 1.8%). On the other hand, prices rose at a faster pace for transport and warehousing (5.01% vs 4.59%), real estate (4.45% vs 4.18%), lodging and food services (2.68% vs 2.61%) and professional, scientific and technical activities (1.57% vs 1.56%), while healthcare prices rose 0.75% after a 0.07% decline in August. Education prices were unchanged at 2.39%, and information and telecommunications prices were flat. Producer prices continued to decline for water supply, sewerage and waste management (-0.15%).

### 2026-10-12 22:16:16  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Tin Rebounds from 3-Month Low — Tin futures rebounded to $53,000 per tonne from the three-month low of $51,450 on October 8th, amid renewed optimism on AI infrastructure, which is expected to drive global tin demand. Strong utilization rates for new AI agents released by Meta drove chipmaker stocks to rally at the turn of October, supporting the outlook for tin. This was further bolstered by reports of potential IPOs from Anthropic and OpenAI, alongside fresh capital-raising rounds by SoftBank and SpaceX, which have invested heavily in infrastructure. Tin consumption remained underpinned by electrification projects and AI infrastructure development due to the metal's broad utility in data centers and battery storage facilities. The metal's critical role in precision soldering across AI hardware prompted industry players to signal that tin demand in AI servers could triple by 2030. Meanwhile, supply from major producer Indonesia remained constrained as Jakarta pulled back on the issuance of export licenses.

Russian Trade Surplus Widens in August — The Russian trade surplus widened to $11.74 billion in August of 2026 from $7.27 billion in the corresponding period of the previous year, the highest for the month since 2022. Exports soared by 27.8% annually to $40.93 billion, supported by higher foreign turnover on energy commodities as the blockade of tankers leaving the Persian Gulf drove global oil and fuel prices to surge, increasing Asian demand for Russian oil alternatives. Energy exports are due to be capped starting September as strikes on Russian refineries by Ukrainian drones drove Moscow to prohibit the export of diesel. Meanwhile, imports rose a softer 17.9% to $29.19 billion.

### 2026-10-12 21:44:02  #公司

【微軟準備在資料中心大規模部署自研晶片 規模達數吉瓦】微軟公司正準備在資料中心部署最新版自研晶片，目標是主要依靠自家硬體為其AI軟體提供算力支援。微軟週一表示，計劃在其資料中心部署總規模達數吉瓦的Maia晶片，其中最新一代Maia 300將從明年開始投入部署。這類晶片專為AI推理而設計，即利用AI模型執行總結電話會議內容或回答研究問題等任務。微軟、亞馬遜和Alphabet旗下谷歌都在開發自研人工智慧加速器，此舉可能有助於降低他們對英偉達硬體的依賴。亞馬遜和谷歌啟動晶片研發的時間早於微軟，被認為在這一領域領先於這家全球最大的軟體公司。微軟押注，透過針對自家資料中心量身定製晶片，可以更高效地執行AI推理，同時減少採購英偉達市場領先的圖形處理器(GPU)所帶來的部分成本。目前，微軟的大部分AI推理工作仍由英偉達GPU提供算力支援。

US Stocks Trade Mixed — US equities were mixed on Monday as strong earnings prospects offset pressure on corporate credit conditions from elevated bond yields. The S&P 500 was flat near its record high and the Dow gained 200 points, while the Nasdaq 100 fell 0.4%. Brokers across major economies signaled buy recommendations in a variety of sectors ahead of the September-quarter earnings season, indicating that companies have sustained strong earnings growth despite a backdrop of higher interest rates. Still, energy prices and bond yields remained higher as the war in the Middle East persists, threatening more hikes by the Federal Reserve this year. Most sectors were higher, with Meta and Salesforce adding 2% and 5%, respectively, in a strong session for software application companies. On the other hand, Micron, AMD, and Marvell fell around 3% each as AI infrastructure companies continued to trade in a volatile manner.

Lithium Falls to Near 10-Month Low — Lithium carbonate prices in China fell to CNY 115,000 per tonne in October, the lowest year-to-date, driven by a combination of softer demand and fresh evidence of a robust supply backdrop. The European Commission noted that this is expected to halve China's market share across the bloc, limiting orders from battery manufacturers in the region after recent data showed higher-than-expected lithium inventories. Industry group SMM changed its methodology for compiling inventory data, which led to a 175,000-tonne increase in Chinese stockpiles, double previous estimates. ### 2026-10-12 20:13:18  #Trading Economics #Economy #Mexico #Industrial Production #Importance 2

Mexico’s Industrial Output Grows the Most in More Than Two Years — Mexico’s industrial production rose 3.5% year-on-year in August 2026, its strongest increase since April 2024, accelerating from 2.7% in July and significantly exceeding market expectations of a 2.6% gain. Growth was driven by stronger manufacturing activity, which accelerated to 3.2% from 1.6% in the previous month, supported by a further increase in the production of petroleum and coal products (21.4% vs. 14.7%). Construction activity also strengthened, rising 7.5% following a 7.3% increase in July, driven by a sharp acceleration in civil engineering construction (17.6% vs. 2.4%). In contrast, mining production declined by 0.8%, reversing a 1.6% increase in the previous month, as mining-related services contracted (-7.3% vs. 26.0%) and petroleum and gas extraction edged down 0.1%, compared with a 1.6% increase in July. On a monthly basis, industrial production rose 0.6%, up from a 0.5% gain in July.

### 2026-10-12 19:50:57  #Trading Economics #Economy #Kosovo #Inflation Rate #Importance 0

Kosovo Inflation Rate Accelerates in September — Kosovo’s annual inflation rate rose to 7.8% in September 2026, the highest since February 2023, from 7.3% in August. Inflation accelerated for transport (23.9% vs 21.1%), alcoholic beverages, tobacco and narcotics (6.3% vs 5.1%), housing and utilities (11.9% vs 11.8%), health (4.5% vs 4.2%), furnishings (3.5% vs 3.4%) and information and communication (0.7% vs 0.6%), while it remained unchanged for clothing and footwear (3%) and personal care and miscellaneous goods and services (3.8%). In contrast, inflation slowed for restaurants and hotels (5.2% vs 5.4%), recreation, sports and culture (1.8% vs 3.4%) and food (2.4% vs 2.6%), easing for the seventh consecutive month. Meanwhile, prices of educational services fell 0.3%, after a 0.5% decline in August. On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 0.2%, easing from a 1.3% increase in August.

### 2026-10-12 19:43:25  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Palladium Trades Near 1-Year Low — Palladium futures traded below $1,200 an ounce, remaining near its lowest level since September 2025, as both near and longer term outlooks remain subdued. Tanker attacks in the Strait of Hormuz lifted oil prices and raised concerns over inflationary pressures, keeping traders cautious and providing some support. However, while markets widely expect the Federal Reserve to leave interest rates unchanged this month, the prospect of a rate hike by year-end could weigh on investment flows into the metal. Over the longer term, the growing adoption of electric vehicles continues to pressure demand for palladium used in catalytic converters, although resilient demand for hybrids has helped moderate the decline. Despite this, the drop in demand continues to underpin a bearish outlook for the metal as it outpaces the contraction in supply, with elevated operating costs and power grid instability in South Africa, alongside ongoing trade risks in Russia, constraining production.

### 2026-10-12 19:07:54  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Cocoa Rises as West African Crop Risks Intensify — Cocoa prices rose above $5,600 a tonne, nearly doubling from the three-year low of $2,888 recorded on February 27, as concerns grew over the next harvest in West Africa. The possible strengthening of El Niño is adding to supply concerns. Ivory Coast, the world’s largest cocoa producer, is expected to face below-average rainfall through early 2027, according to the country’s weather agency. West Africa accounts for more than half of global cocoa production, leaving international supplies particularly exposed to adverse weather conditions in the region. StoneX Group expects Ivory Coast’s output to fall 11% to 1.8 million tonnes in the 2026–27 season. Ghana, the second-largest producer, also expects production to decline by around 13% to 650,000 tonnes. Meanwhile, estimates showed cocoa arrivals at Ivorian ports reached 77,500 tonnes so far since the start of the 2026/27 season.

India Inflation Rises to 2-Year High — The headline inflation rate in India rose to 5.54% in September 2026 from 4.83% in the previous month, the highest since October 2024 and ahead of market expectations of 5.4%. It was the 11th straight month of rising inflation in the economy, approaching the Reserve Bank of India's upper tolerance limit of 6% as adverse weather and geopolitical shocks to oil prices lifted the costs of major products in the Indian consumer basket. Food costs were 7.74% higher year-on-year, with onion prices jumping 108% while ginger (62.9%) and garlic (54.6%) also increased sharply. Meanwhile, higher oil prices and a weakening rupee drove transportation inflation to 5.01%. Price growth was also high for restaurants and accommodation (9.36%) and personal care and social protection (13.75%). Month-on-month, the Indian CPI rose by 0.69%.

For the January–August period, the trade deficit widened to EUR 6,303.3 million from EUR 5,483.5 million in the same period of 2025.

### 2026-10-12 17:10:15  #Trading Economics #Economy #Kazakhstan #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

Kazakhstan Producer Inflation Picks Up in September — Producer price inflation in Kazakhstan accelerated to 6.4% in September 2026, up from 5.6% in August. Price growth surged in the supply of electricity, gas, steam, hot water, and air conditioning, reaching 21.9% from 12% in the previous month. Inflation also picked up in mining and quarrying, rising to 5.7% from 5.1%. In contrast, producer price inflation eased in manufacturing (5% vs. 5.5%) and in water supply, sewerage, waste collection and treatment, and remediation activities (8% vs. 8.7%). From the previous month, producer prices rose by 1%, slowing from a 1.2% increase in August.

### 2026-10-12 17:09:15  #Trading Economics #Markets #France #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

French Bond Yields Little-Changed as Fiscal Concerns Persist — France’s 10-year OAT yield hovered around 4.86%, easing slightly from a more than two-decade high as lower oil prices prompted markets to scale back expectations for further European Central Bank tightening. However, French bonds remained under pressure amid concerns over the country’s record-high debt and political obstacles to fiscal consolidation. The government’s 2027 budget aims to reduce the fiscal deficit to 5% of GDP, but securing parliamentary approval for substantial spending cuts remains uncertain amid a deeply divided legislature and next year’s presidential election. During October’s Eurogroup discussions, EU officials urged Paris to present a credible budget and meet its commitments under the EU fiscal framework. French lawmakers are set to begin debating the budget’s revenue measures this week. Investors are also watching Wednesday’s US CPI report and ECB policymakers’ remarks at the IMF-World Bank annual meetings for clues on the monetary policy outlook.

Crude Oil Rises for 3rd Day — Crude oil traded above $92.5 a barrel on Monday, attempting gains for the third session, as escalating Middle East tensions heightened concerns over regional security. Saudi Arabia’s main airport came under attack again on Sunday, while another tanker was targeted in the Strait of Hormuz. The incidents followed a phone call between Saudi and Iranian foreign ministers and came amid renewed fighting involving Iran-backed Houthi forces in Yemen. Despite the security risks, oil exports from the Persian Gulf remained relatively resilient. Shipments reportedly recovered to 17.5 million bpd, around 98% of pre-war levels. Meanwhile, US President Donald Trump claimed that Russia and Ukraine agreed to an immediate energy ceasefire. Moscow plans to export 500,000 tons of diesel, far below last year’s 28.6 million tons. The IEA urged governments to accelerate pledged diesel releases, warning that current market conditions require immediate attention ahead of its October 15 meeting.

Aluminum Falls to Lowest Since February — Aluminum futures in the UK were at $3,050 per tonne, hovering at their lowest level since February, weighed down by a stronger US dollar, easing concerns over Gulf supply disruptions, and expanding production capacity in Indonesia. Macquarie recently lowered its forecast for the global aluminum supply deficit as smelter restarts in the Middle East progressed faster than expected. In late August, Emirates Global Aluminium said that a quarter of its Al Taweelah smelter in Abu Dhabi had resumed operations as recovery efforts continued following damage from an Iranian attack in March. In addition, Rio Tinto secured an agreement to keep its Bell Bay Aluminium smelter in Tasmania operational through the end of 2031, further boosting supply prospects. Meanwhile, the US dollar hovered near an 18-month high, putting further pressure on dollar-denominated commodities. Weaker demand expectations in China also weighed on LME aluminum prices to some extent.

Bund Yields Ease — Germany’s 10-year Bund yield slipped to 3.46%, near its lowest level in over a week, as easing oil prices and reduced concerns over Middle Eastern supply disruptions prompted investors to scale back expectations for further ECB tightening. Safe-haven demand also supported German government bonds. European bond yields nevertheless remain elevated amid economic resilience and persistent energy-driven inflationary pressures. Investors are now awaiting Wednesday’s US CPI report and remarks from ECB policymakers at this week’s IMF-World Bank annual meetings for clues on the monetary policy outlook. Meanwhile, concerns over France’s public finances persist as the government seeks to reduce its deficit to 5% of GDP in 2027, despite uncertainty over parliamentary support for spending cuts ahead of next year’s presidential election. German politics also drew attention after Bärbel Bas announced her resignation as Labour Minister and SPD leader.

Euro Nears 17-Month Low on French Debt Concerns — The euro began the week trading below $1.12, hovering near its weakest level in 17 months, as investors weighed France’s record-high debt and political challenges to implementing budget cuts against the resilience of the US economy and the strength of the dollar. France’s budget plans for 2027 aim to reduce the fiscal deficit to 5% of GDP, but concerns are mounting over whether the government can secure parliamentary approval for substantial spending cuts amid a deeply divided parliament, particularly with next year’s presidential election approaching. Meanwhile, Brent crude prices edged lower as easing concerns over supply risks prompted traders to scale back expectations for further central bank rate hikes. Markets are also looking ahead to Wednesday’s US CPI data for clues about the Federal Reserve’s interest-rate outlook, while eurozone central bankers are scheduled to deliver speeches during the IMF-World Bank annual meetings in Bangkok this week.

### 2026-10-12 16:51:18  #宏觀 #公司 #市場 #焦點

【證監會：探索更加適配創新型中小企業的制度和實踐】證監會辦公廳副主任王利10月12日在2026金融街論壇年會新聞釋出會上表示，五年來，證監會紮實推動市場高質量發展，北交所執行安全穩定、功能有效發揮，市場建設取得積極成效。

一是有效支援中小企業創新發展。

二是切實提升市場活力和韌性。堅持投融資兩端協調發展，大力加強北交所投資端建設，引導投早、投小、投長期、投硬科技，增強市場執行內在穩定性。現有合格投資者近1170萬戶，較開市時增加兩倍，超過3000只公募基金配置北交所股票，機構投資者交易佔比由開市時的9%提升至目前的24%。今年以來日均成交額超過200億元，較開市初期有大幅增長，市場估值和流動性水平持續改善。目前，北交所主題基金、北證50指數基金共有58只，存續基金規模超過160億元，吸引長期資金入市的產品體系日益完善。加強投資者回報，開市以來九成以上北交所上市公司實施分紅，累計分紅270億元，168家公司連續三年分紅，分紅穩定性和可預期性持續增強。

三是積極構建股債聯動市場格局。堅持“股債並舉”建設思路，推動債券市場高標準起步、高質量發展。政府債方面，實現交易所市場國債常態化發行，累計發行國債超過18萬億元，約佔全國發行總量的三分之一，有效助力國家宏觀調控；發行地方政府債1.5萬億元，覆蓋22個省市，積極支援區域經濟發展。信用債方面，推出科創債、綠色債、中小微企業支援債等10個專項債品種，累計完成發行融資超過270億元，拓寬了科技創新企業融資渠道，形成股債雙輪驅動格局。

四是持續完善服務中小企業發展的良好市場生態。

王利表示，下一步，證監會將按照“十五五”規劃《綱要》部署，立足多層次資本市場建設整體格局，推動北交所、新三板一體化高質量發展，探索更加適配創新型中小企業的制度和實踐，深化改革創新，豐富產品體系，強化功能發揮，更好助力金融強國建設和經濟高質量發展。 (證券時報)

Bulgaria Trade Deficit Smallest in One Year — Bulgaria’s trade deficit widened to EUR 678.7 million in August 2026 from EUR 550.5 million in the same month a year earlier. Still, it marked the smallest trade shortfall since August 2025, as exports rose 20.68% year-on-year to EUR 4,032.2 million, driven by stronger sales to EU countries, which increased 25.63%, and non-EU markets, which grew 12.68%. Meanwhile, imports climbed 21.32% to EUR 4,710.9 million, driven by higher purchases from non-EU countries, which rose 36.81%, and EU countries, which increased 8.97%. In the first eight months of 2026, Bulgaria’s trade deficit widened to EUR 8,073.7 million from EUR 5,920.4 million in the corresponding period of the previous year.

【ING: Limited further upside for Swiss franc vs euro】ING strategist Chris Turner says the Swiss franc has recently strengthened versus the euro but further gains are likely limited because market expectations for ECB tightening are unlikely to be pared back materially. The franc's move was mainly driven by concerns over French sovereign debt that cut market expectations for ECB tightening. "It is unclear whether short-term euro rates need to fall further materially, unless energy prices decline significantly," Turner wrote. Euro-area inflation remains elevated, he added, giving the ECB scope to tighten policy further.

【寒武紀：公司的主要產品線包括雲端產品線、邊緣產品線、IP授權及軟體】有投資者向寒武紀（688256.SH）提問，近期市場有訊息稱公司當前月投產規模約7萬顆晶片，請問是否屬實？如果真的有這麼多產能，該產能是否主要是邊端線產品？目前貴公司邊端主力仍為MLU220，而該產品早在2019年釋出，距今已超過6年，寒武紀是否重新關注邊端AI計算市場？10月12日，公司回答表示，公司持續推動智慧晶片產品、基礎系統軟體平臺的迭代升級與最佳化，提升公司晶片產品的市場競爭力，為客戶提供優質的晶片產品。公司的主要產品線包括雲端產品線、邊緣產品線、IP授權及軟體。2026年上半年度，公司憑藉出色的產品力持續拓展市場，積極助力人工智慧應用落地，其中，雲端產品線實現營業收入59.94億元，較上年同期大幅增長。未來，在大模型技術革新的背景下，公司將聚焦核心應用場景，強化市場開拓力度，推動公司整體業務的發展和市場份額的提升，鞏固公司在人工智慧晶片領域的領先地位。公司資訊請以公司釋出在上交所官網的公告或公司官方網站披露的資訊為準。敬請投資者提高資訊辨別能力，不傳播、不採信來源不明或未經核實的資訊。

### 2026-10-12 16:15:30  #公司

【新特電氣：有序推進移相變壓器在資料中心的應用】近日，有投資者在互動平臺向新特電氣提問：公司作為生產SST（固態變壓器）核心部件的廠商，下游客戶都有哪些？跟阿里資料中心合作進展順利嗎？10月12日，新特電氣回覆稱， 公司中高頻隔離變壓器等磁性器件的下游主要為電力電子系統整合，涵蓋資料中心供配電領域頭部企業等。目前，公司有序推進移相變壓器在資料中心的應用，有關業務進展情況可關注公司後續在深交所指定資訊披露平臺披露的定期報告及相關臨時公告。此前，新特電氣在互動平臺回覆投資者提問時稱，公司主營各類特種變壓器及儲能相關產品，其中變壓器包括變頻用變壓器、船用變壓器、氫能配套油變式變壓器、電力變壓器等特種變壓器以及中頻隔離直流變壓器、中壓高頻變壓器等磁性器件，儲能產品包括儲能澆注變壓器、集中式儲能變流器、組串式儲能變流器、EMS、交流升壓一體機、工商業儲能系統、大型儲能系統等產品。其中，中頻直流變壓器已有交付案例，中壓高頻變壓器在測試階段。新特電氣表示，公司是國內最早研發和生產變頻用變壓器的企業之一，依託長期電力電子技術積澱，公司目前研發的中壓高頻變壓器為固態變壓器（SST）的核心器件，具備高絕緣性、低局放性等特點，可應用於資料中心場景。

【Madison Investment: 10-year US yield driven by multiple factors, can signal Fed policy effectiveness】Madison Investment head of fixed income Mike Sanders said investors closely watch the 10-year US Treasury yield as the benchmark risk-free rate underpinning global asset valuations. Traditionally it reflects growth and inflation expectations, but Sanders warned its drivers now also include the Fed’s policy path, energy prices, fiscal deficits and foreign investor demand. He added that when the Fed adjusts short-term policy rates to fight inflation or stimulate growth, the market-determined 10-year yield often indicates how effective that policy has been.

### 2026-10-12 16:11:52  #MKT News

【China Ministry of Education to launch vocational-education plan to boost service-sector capacity and quality】China’s Ministry of Education will roll out a vocational-education work plan aimed at expanding and upgrading service-sector capacity by prioritizing high-skilled talent. The plan will target eight priority fields chosen for large capacity, strong growth support, clear GDP pull and acute skills shortages, and sets a task ledger covering teaching-factor reform, vocational skills training and vocational education standards with responsibility-based, checklist implementation. For technical support services it will focus on high-end equipment clusters in Jing-Jin-Ji, Chang-Zhu-Tan and Chengdu–Chongqing, prioritizing high-skilled training in aerospace equipment and intelligent non-destructive testing for rail transit. For software and data services it will target the Greater Bay Area, key central and western cities and national data-labeling pilot cities, emphasizing large-scale domestic software adoption, AI-related needs and training in HarmonyOS application and human-in-the-loop data labeling.

【India coal stockpiles plunge, sparking power crisis; factories, smelters and households face outages and higher energy costs】Coal inventories at India’s thermal power plants have plunged, triggering a power squeeze that is causing outages and rising energy costs. This season’s monsoon—the weakest since 2015—has cut hydro output, while exceptional heat has lifted air‑conditioning and irrigation demand, accelerating burn rates at coal plants that supply roughly two‑thirds of India’s electricity. With demand outstripping supply, small factories, farmers and households are facing load curtailments. Large industrial users including metal smelters, chemical and pulp‑and‑paper plants are unable to secure enough coal for captive generation because state miner Coal India Ltd is prioritizing deliveries to grid‑connected power stations.

【今年公司晶片交付進展如何？寒武紀回應】有投資者向寒武紀（688256.SH）提問，今年公司晶片交付進展如何，如何評價公司股價今年以來大跌25%，大幅跌破增發價？有無小作文誘空，麻煩公司正面回應。10月12日，公司回答表示，公司憑藉出色的產品力持續拓展市場，積極助力人工智慧應用落地，2025年度，公司實現營業收入649,719.62萬元，首次實現了全年利潤的扭虧為盈。2026年上半年度，公司實現營業收入599,557.36萬元，較上年同期增長108.13%；本期營業收入實現大幅增長的同時，歸屬於上市公司股東的淨利潤達到231,091.21萬元，較上年同期增長122.61%。未來，在大模型技術革新的背景下，公司將聚焦核心應用場景，強化市場開拓力度，推動公司整體業務的發展和市場份額的提升，鞏固公司在人工智慧晶片領域的領先地位。二級市場股價會受到宏觀經濟、行業政策、市場環境等綜合因素影響，敬請投資者理性看待市場波動並注意投資風險。此外，市場資訊繁雜，敬請投資者提高資訊辨別能力，不傳播、不採信來源不明或未經核實的資訊，以公司釋出在上交所官網的公告或公司官方網站披露的資訊為準。

French Stocks Rise to Start the Week — The CAC 40 rose 0.6% to 7,864 on Monday, recovering from losses in the previous week following US President Donald Trump’s remarks on an “energy ceasefire.” Oil prices fell after Trump said Russia and Ukraine had agreed to an immediate ceasefire targeting energy infrastructure, while Ukrainian President Volodymyr Zelenskyy said Kyiv supports the truce but is “awaiting details.” However, lingering concerns over escalating tensions in the Middle East continued to temper risk appetite after a deadly attack on Riyadh’s airport prompted Trump to consider joining Saudi Arabia in retaliatory action against Houthi militants in Yemen. ### 2026-10-12 15:19:25  #Trading Economics #Economy #Russia #Government Budget Value #Importance 0

Russia Government Budget Gap Widens in January-September — The Russian federal government recorded a budget deficit of RUB 5.2 trillion in January-September 2026, equivalent to 2.2% of GDP, up from RUB 3.5 trillion in the same period a year earlier, according to preliminary estimates from the Finance Ministry. The shortfall remains below the full-year target of RUB 7.3 trillion, or 3.2% of GDP. Federal expenditures climbed by 14.5% year-on-year to RUB 34.825 trillion, while revenues rose by 10% to RUB 29.639 trillion. Non-oil-and-gas revenues increased by 18.8% to RUB 24.168 trillion, partly offsetting a 17.2% decline in oil and gas revenues to RUB 5.471 trillion. The ministry attributed the drop in energy revenue partly to a lower average dollar exchange rate during the period and higher payments under the fuel price damping mechanism.

### 2026-10-12 15:17:26  #Trading Economics #Economy #Turkey #Retail Sales YoY #Importance 1

Turkey Retail Sales Pick Up in August — Retail sales in Turkey rose 10.1% year-on-year in August 2026, accelerating from a downwardly revised and two-year low of 9.9% in the previous month. Sales growth accelerated at non-specialized stores (5.6% vs 3.6% in July), specialized food, beverage and tobacco stores (4% vs 3%), information and communication equipment stores (11.6% vs 9.4%), and non-store retail trade (17.7% vs 16.8%). In contrast, trade slowed for automotive fuel (6.9% vs 8.1%), cultural and recreational goods (8.9% vs 9.5%), other goods in specialized stores (19.8% vs 20.2%), and sales through stalls and markets (9.7% vs 17.4%). Moreover, sales of other household equipment fell 2.7%, reversing a 0.2% increase in July. On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, retail sales rose 1.4% in August, the strongest increase in three months, following a 0.2% gain in July.

India 10Y Yield Hits 3-Year High — The yield on India’s 10-year G-Sec rose more than 1% to 7.38%, reaching its highest level in over three years, as the Reserve Bank of India’s liquidity-tightening measures and hawkish policy shift intensified selling pressure. The central bank plans to sell INR 250 billion in bonds on Tuesday and raise banks’ daily cash reserve requirement maintenance to 99% from 90%, effective October 16, while its foreign-exchange measures could further drain rupee liquidity. The RBI’s recent 25-basis-point rate hike and signals of further tightening have also prompted mutual funds to reduce exposure to longer-dated government bonds, following net sales of INR 378 billion in July-September and a further INR 51 billion in the three sessions after the rate increase. Meanwhile, Brent crude above USD 100 per barrel and expectations of September inflation rising to 5.4% year-on-year added to concerns over price pressures.

UK Natural Gas Prices Rise to 4-Week High — UK natural gas prices rose to around 203 pence per therm on Monday, a four-week high, as increased tanker attacks in the Strait of Hormuz and escalating regional tensions heightened concerns over the security of LNG supplies. The strait handles around 20% of global LNG trade before the war. Despite a steady rise in European gas inventories in recent weeks, storage facilities are around 73% full, still below year-ago levels. However, this recovery could be threatened by forecasts of cooler autumn temperatures, which may lead to an earlier start to the heating season and increase gas consumption. Last week, European gas network operators said the system was flexible enough to meet winter demand but warned that limited supplies could push storage below 30% by winter’s end, increasing vulnerability to late-season cold snaps. Meanwhile, growing renewable output in the UK has reduced dependence on gas-fired electricity, but the country remains exposed to fuel price swings.

Iron Ore Stabilizes on Chinese Steel Mill Restocking Hopes — Iron ore futures climbed toward CNY 690 per ton, recovering some ground after hitting four-year lows last week, as expectations of restocking by Chinese steelmakers following an extended holiday provided support, although weak market fundamentals continued to limit gains. More mills are expected to return to the spot market this week to replenish raw material inventories and maintain production levels. Meanwhile, lower spot coke prices helped ease cost pressures and narrow steelmakers’ losses, potentially slowing the pace of production cuts. However, signs of rising global supply capped the upside, as miners increased shipments to meet quarterly and annual targets in the fourth quarter. Recent data also revealed that only 6.9% of Chinese steelmakers were profitable at the end of September, highlighting persistent challenges across the sector.

### 2026-10-12 14:49:59  #其他

【德意志銀行：科技板塊短期吸引力下降】德意志銀行策略師表示，市場持倉結構仍不均衡，投資者超配科技板塊，資金輪入該板塊推動股價逼近長期趨勢上限。由帕拉格·塔特牽頭的策略團隊寫道，上週大型成長股與科技股持倉有所回落，但依舊處於超配狀態；消費週期板塊升至小幅超配，其餘板塊為低配。報告稱整體股票持倉小幅下降，但仍為小幅超配。報告指出，自由裁量型投資者維持小幅低配股票；量化策略持倉從高位回落，但依舊超配。在量化策略內部，波動控制型基金股票配置提升，而商品交易顧問基金股票持倉大幅下降，不過仍處在高位。股票型基金資金流入放緩（新增124億美元），債券型基金流入規模躍升至四個月新高（新增338億美元），貨幣市場基金取得逾6.5年來最大資金流入（新增1664億美元）。

【科技大跌誰在砸盤？百億級私募創始人暗示】近期，科技板塊持續調整，誰在砸盤？私募大佬指向部分機構追漲殺跌。“這不是基本面原因，而是資金面原因。我們瞭解到某些保險資管巨頭在七月份高位買了很多科技股，卻在九月下旬大幅度減倉止損。這種大機構追漲殺跌的操作實在令人費解。”百億私募大佬梁宏在最新的週報中表示。梁宏在週報裡寫道：“我認為這主要非基本面原因，而是資金面原因。AI發展的很好很順利前景依然很大，但是國內資金面情況不好，的確出乎我意料。我們瞭解到某些保險資管巨頭在七月份高位買了很多科技股，卻在九月下旬大幅度減倉止損。這種大機構追漲殺跌的操作實在令人費解。”在梁宏看來，當前科技板塊基本面很好，當前股價越來越便宜，意味著未來空間越來越大。“如果基本面不好，美股幾個GPU、CPU和晶圓製造龍頭也不會創新高，以AH便宜和確定性好的公司未來空間會很大。”（上證報）

【港交所：香港的固定收益和大宗商品生態圈建設應該把握好人民幣國際化帶來的機遇】10月12日，香港交易所集團行政總裁陳翊庭釋出新一期CEO網誌。任何一個國際金融中心要想吸引和留住海量的資金，都必須提供儘量豐富的“貨架”，不單要有琳琅滿目的股票產品，也要配套豐富多元的固定收益產品和大宗商品。股票產品是香港的傳統優勢，但固定收益和大宗商品業務卻剛剛起步，需要大力投入。與此同時，人民幣國際化正在進入一個新的階段，提升人民幣作為投融資貨幣的功能為重點，如果香港市場能夠利用離岸人民幣資金池提供豐富的以人民幣計價的固定收益、貨幣和大宗商品產品，必然會提升國際投資者長期持有人民幣資產的意願，也會提升國際金融中心的吸引力。因此，香港的固定收益和大宗商品生態圈建設應該把握好人民幣國際化帶來的機遇。在建設固定收益及貨幣市場方面，香港證監會與香港金融管理局去年聯合釋出了香港的《固定收益及貨幣市場發展路線圖》，旨在把香港打造成為全球固定收益及貨幣中心，鞏固香港作為全球離岸人民幣樞紐的核心地位。香港交易所的工作計劃完全契合《路線圖》方向，我們要當好執行路線圖的“施工隊”，全力落實路線圖中的倡議，完善離岸人民幣生態圈。（證券時報）

### 2026-10-12 14:33:51  #Trading Economics #Economy #Romania #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Romania Trade Deficit Largest in 20 Months — Romania’s trade deficit widened to EUR 3.138 billion in August 2026 from EUR 2.635 billion in the corresponding month a year earlier, reaching its largest level since December 2024. Exports increased 11.6% year-on-year to EUR 7.554 billion, supported by higher shipments to both EU (14.5%) and non-EU countries (5.1%). Meanwhile, imports rose 13.7% to EUR 10.692 billion, driven by higher purchases from both EU (11.5%) and non-EU markets (18.5%). During the January-August period, exports increased 4.0% to EUR 65.825 billion, driven by higher sales of machinery and transport equipment (2.1%), manufactured goods classified mainly by raw materials (7.1%), and food and live animals (9.8%). Meanwhile, imports rose 3.4% to EUR 88.270 billion, with higher arrivals of machinery and transport equipment (4.5%), chemicals and related products (5.7%), and mineral fuels, lubricants, and related products (14.5%). Over this period, the country’s trade deficit totaled EUR 22.445 billion.

### 2026-10-12 14:15:56  #Trading Economics #Economy #Denmark #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Denmark Inflation Rises to 1-Year High — Denmark’s annual inflation rate rose to 2.2% in September 2026, the highest level in a year, from 2.0% in August. The main upward pressures came from higher housing and utility costs (1.7% vs 1.0%) and a pickup in transport inflation (7.7% vs 4.5%). Price increases were also recorded in recreation, sports and culture (2.7% vs 2.3%), personal care and miscellaneous goods and services (2.0% vs 1.8%), and household equipment and services edged up (0.1% vs -0.2%). In contrast, price growth slowed for restaurants and hotels (5.2% vs 7.6%) and information and communication (3.7% vs 5.1%). Food prices fell further (-2.1% vs. -1.5%), while clothing and footwear prices also dropped (-0.6% vs 1.1%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices were unchanged in September, after falling 0.3% in the previous month. Core inflation, which excludes energy and unprocessed food, eased to 1.9% in September, its lowest level in five months, from 2.2% in August.

Palm Oil Rises Despite Bearish MPOB Data — Malaysian palm oil futures jumped 1.3% to about MYR 4,650 per tonne, rebounding from earlier weakness as firmer prices on China’s Dalian market lent support. Export prospects also brightened after surveyor Intertek estimated Malaysia’s shipments surged 33.5% in the first ten days of October from the same period in September. In top buyer India, refiners reportedly booked around 150,000 tonnes of palm oil in three days last week, with sunflower oil supplies tight ahead of November’s festive demand. Even so, upside was capped by bearish monthly data from the Malaysian Palm Oil Board. September inventories swelled 24.5% to a record 3.51 million tonnes, extending a six-month rise. At the same time, output climbed 16.5% to 2.12million tonnes, the highest since August 2015, while exports fell 12.1% to 1.12million tonnes, marking a second monthly drop. Traders remained cautious, awaiting China’s inflation and trade figures later this week for further signals on demand in another key market.

Estonia Trade Deficit Widens in August — Estonia’s trade deficit widened to EUR 360 million in August 2026 from EUR 249 million in the same month last year. Exports increased 3% year-on-year to EUR 1,475 million, boosted by higher sales to EU countries (+7%), while shipments to non-EU countries declined (-8%). Exports rose most notably for base metals and articles thereof (+18%), raw materials and products of the chemical industry (+17%), paper and articles thereof (+13%), and optical, measuring and precision instruments (+8%). Meanwhile, imports advanced at a much faster pace, rising 10% to EUR 1,835 million, driven by higher purchases from both EU (+10%) and non-EU (+8%) countries. Notable increases were recorded in mineral products (+30%), machinery and mechanical appliances (+25%), raw materials and products of the chemical industry (+18%), and transport equipment (+17%). In the first eight months of the year, Estonia recorded a trade deficit of EUR 2,701 million, up from EUR 2,561 million in the same period of 2025.

Dutch Trade Surplus Widens in August — The Netherlands’ trade surplus widened to EUR 8.3 billion in August 2026 from EUR 7.5 billion in the same month a year earlier. Exports rose 13.2% year-on-year to EUR 68.8 billion, as shipments increased to both EU (+12.4%) and non-EU (+14.4%) countries. Among commodities, exports grew the most for mineral fuels and lubricants (+42.5%), machinery and transport equipment (+27.2%), beverages and tobacco (+10.5%), and animal and vegetable oils (+9.3%). Meanwhile, imports also rose 13.2% to EUR 60.5 billion, driven by higher purchases from EU (+8.2%) and non-EU (+17.4%) markets. Arrivals increased notably for mineral fuels and lubricants (+39.1%), machinery and transport equipment (+18.4%), raw materials (+17.4%), and chemical products (+16.2%). In the January–August period, the Dutch trade surplus stood at EUR 75.6 billion, as exports rose 4.7% from the same period last year, while imports increased 5.7%.

### 2026-10-12 12:35:20  #Trading Economics #Markets #Indonesia #Currency #Importance 1

Rupiah Holds Range as Domestic and External Risks Persist — The Indonesian rupiah traded near IDR 17,900 per U.S. dollar on Monday, moving within a limited band as mixed domestic signals and external risks kept sentiment cautious. Retail sales posted their fastest growth in five months in August, but consumer confidence slipped from a recent high. Inflationary pressures also persisted after inflation hit a three-month high in September, with food costs likely to sustain upward pressure through year-end. Foreign exchange reserves eased a bit, but remained near a five-month high. However, Bank Indonesia faces a growing policy trade-off between supporting economic growth and safeguarding the currency. A persistent current account deficit and tighter global financing conditions leave the rupiah vulnerable to shifts in foreign capital flows. Globally, the dollar index remained firm ahead of U.S. inflation data, while renewed Houthi attacks on Saudi Arabia heightened geopolitical uncertainty and added to market caution.

### 2026-10-12 12:23:04  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Copper Gains on Supply Disruptions — Copper futures climbed toward $6.70 per pound on Monday, moving closer to record highs as ongoing supply disruptions bolstered prices. In Chile, the world’s leading copper producer, two workers died on Sunday after a snow avalanche struck Codelco’s massive El Teniente mine, where open-pit operations had already been suspended due to adverse weather conditions. Meanwhile, a strike at Antofagasta’s Centinela mine is expected to reduce output from one of the world’s largest copper operations during the second half of October. On the demand front, surging orders linked to data centers, semiconductor development, and battery storage for AI technologies and electric vehicles across Asia continued to strengthen the outlook for copper. The metal also gained support from falling oil prices, which could ease inflationary pressures and reduce the need for aggressive monetary tightening by the US Federal Reserve.

### 2026-10-12 11:58:09  #Trading Economics #Markets #New Zealand #Currency #Importance 1

New Zealand Dollar Pinned Near Multi-Month Lows — The New Zealand dollar slipped to $0.560, hovering near its lowest level since November 2025, amid a sluggish economy and growing interest rate differentials between New Zealand and other major nations. The kiwi faces greater pressure than some other currencies as New Zealand’s economic recovery remains fragile and although the Reserve Bank raised interest rates at its past two policy meetings this year, the official cash rate remains below those of several overseas counterparts, making New Zealand dollar-denominated assets less attractive to international investors. At the same time, escalating tensions in the Middle East are pressuring the currencies of economies exposed to higher energy costs. Uncertainty surrounding New Zealand’s general election in November has also added to concerns over the economic outlook, further weighing on sentiment toward the kiwi.

### 2026-10-12 11:55:02  #Trading Economics #Markets #India #Currency #Importance 2

Rupee Rebounds on Fresh RBI Measures — The Indian rupee strengthened to around 95.77 per dollar, rebounding from near all-time lows as the Reserve Bank of India (RBI) introduced additional measures to curb depreciation pressures. Effective October 12, the central bank opened a special window to meet the entire daily dollar requirements of three state-owned oil companies, reducing a significant source of demand in the spot foreign exchange market. The RBI also tightened rules on foreign-exchange derivatives to curb speculative corporate activity and introduced a cash reserve requirement for current account transactions exceeding USD 2 million, raising the cost of hedging against further rupee weakness. The currency had fallen last week to 96.73 per dollar on Friday, approaching its all-time low of 96.96 hit in May.

South Korean Won Hits 2-Year High — The South Korean won traded around 1,333 per dollar, reaching its strongest level since October 2024, as robust export growth and dollar selling by local companies supported the currency. South Korea’s exports more than doubled year-on-year to a record USD 26.1 billion in the first 10 days of October, led by a 170.9% surge in semiconductor shipments to USD 12.1 billion, reinforcing the favorable external backdrop. Additionally, chip exporters’ conversion of overseas earnings into won further supported the currency. Oil prices also eased on hopes that increased Russian diesel supplies could help relieve global fuel shortages, after President Trump said Moscow had agreed to release diesel to global markets and claimed Russia and Ukraine had reached an immediate energy ceasefire. Meanwhile, elevated US Treasury yields and the prospect of further Fed tightening continued to limit the won’s gains. Renewed Houthi attacks on Saudi Arabia over the weekend also added to geopolitical risks.

Yen Holds Steady in Thin Holiday Trade — The Japanese yen traded around 158.2 per dollar on Monday, with trading activity expected to remain subdued due to a national holiday in Japan and a bank holiday in the US. Nevertheless, traders remained cautious as the currency hovered near the key 160-per-dollar threshold, a level that previously prompted Tokyo to intervene in support of the yen. The yen continued to face downward pressure from the wide interest rate differential between the US and Japan, amid relatively low domestic borrowing costs and the country’s substantial debt burden. Meanwhile, uncertainty persisted over whether the Bank of Japan would raise interest rates again this month, although markets continued to anticipate a possible move in December or January. ### 2026-10-12 10:47:55  #Trading Economics #Markets #Indonesia #Stock Market #Importance 1

Indonesian Shares Trade Lower After Recent Rally — Indonesian stocks fell 0.3% to 6,077 in Monday morning trade, easing after a prior rally as infrastructure, financials, and transport weighed. Sentiment turned cautious after U.S. President Donald Trump said he may join Saudi Arabia in retaliating against Tehran-backed Houthis following a deadly attack on Riyadh’s main airport over the weekend. Traders also vigilantly awaited CPI/PPI and trade data from main trading partner China due later this week as activity resumed after a major break. Locally, inflation risks stayed in focus after September figures hit a three-month high, with food prices expected to remain a key driver through year-end. Still, losses were limited by August retail sales, which posted their fastest growth in five months amid government measures to cushion households against cost pressures. Notable laggards included Gojek Tokopedia (-6.7%), XL Smart Telecom (-3.4%), Mitra Adiperkasa (-2.3%), and Indika Energy (-2.2%).

### 2026-10-12 10:41:44  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Corn Futures Drop on Rising Stockpiles — Corn futures held below $4.9 per bushel, after dropping over 4% in the previous session to a seven-week low as the USDA's October report projected a larger US harvest and higher ending stocks for the 2026/27 season. The agency raised its yield estimate to 181.2 bushels per acre from 178.5 in September, lifting production to around 16.03 billion bushels. Ending stocks were also raised to 1.849 billion bushels from the previous estimate, reinforcing concerns over ample supplies. Although the USDA increased its export forecast by 25 million bushels to 3.3 billion, the upward revision in production outweighed stronger projected demand. The agency also lowered its season-average farm price forecast to $4.70 per bushel from $4.80. Meanwhile, US corn harvesting was 23% complete as of October 4, below the five-year average of 27%. Rain was expected to return to parts of the Plains and Midwest during October 12–16, potentially slowing fieldwork and delaying farmer selling.

【RBI rolls out aggressive FX-support measures; offshore rupee gains】Offshore rupee strengthened after the Reserve Bank of India unveiled one of its largest FX-support packages since the 2013 taper tantrum. In early Asian trading, one‑month USD/INR NDF fell as much as 2.7% to 94.17 before trimming to a 0.7% decline. Announced Saturday, measures include a special dollar window for state oil firms and reserve requirements on certain FX derivatives. The moves follow the rupee’s slide toward a near-record low around 97 per dollar and a policy rate hike by the RBI last Wednesday, when it signalled potential for further tightening. SOCGEN’s Hong Kong emerging-Asia strategist Galvin Zia said the package reflects an overdue escalation in FX defence. Market participants expect the rupee to resume appreciation when onshore trading reopens and see the RBI defending the c.97/USD level.

### 2026-10-12 10:25:29  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Soybeans Fall on Higher US Supply Outlook — Soybean futures fell below $12.9 per bushel, reaching a one-week low as the USDA's latest report pointed to a more abundant US supply outlook for the 2026/27 season. The agency raised its soybean yield estimate to 53.1 bushels per acre from 52.8 in September, lifting projected production to 4.56 billion bushels from 4.535 billion. US ending stocks were also revised higher to 315 million bushels from 310 million, adding to concerns over ample supplies. However, the USDA increased its export forecast by 10 million bushels, providing some offset to the bearish outlook, while its season-average farm price estimate remained unchanged at $12 per bushel. Meanwhile, China's Agriculture Ministry cut its 2026/27 soybean output forecast by 590,000 tonnes to 20.36 million tonnes in its October outlook, citing lower-than-expected planted area and yields. The reduction, alongside higher production costs and rising global prices, supported new-crop soybean prices in China.

【外交部：對貝里斯外交、外貿、教育、文化、科學和技術部長豐塞卡採取反制措施】外交部10月12日釋出關於對貝里斯外交、外貿、教育、文化、科學和技術部長豐塞卡採取反制措施的決定：近年來，貝里斯個別反華政客出於一己私利，頑固支援貝里斯同中國臺灣地區保持所謂“邦交”關係，在涉華問題上採取一系列惡劣言行，損害中方核心利益。為捍衛國家主權、安全、發展利益，反對“臺獨”分裂行徑，依據《中華人民共和國反外國制裁法》第三條、第四條、第六條、第九條、第十五條之規定，中方決定對貝里斯外交、外貿、教育、文化、科學和技術部長豐塞卡採取以下反制措施：一、凍結其在我國境內的動產、不動產和其他各類財產；二、禁止我國境內的組織、個人與其進行交易、合作等活動；三、對其不予簽發簽證、不準入境（包括香港、澳門）。本決定自2026年10月12日起施行。附件：反制清單反制清單弗朗西斯·豐塞卡（Francis Fonseca），男，貝里斯籍。（外交部）

### 2026-10-12 10:17:35  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

US Natural Gas Prices Ease — US natural gas futures fell to $3.15 per MMBtu on Monday after rising to a two-week high in the previous session on supply disruptions in the Gulf of Mexico caused by Hurricane Isaias. Producers shut in around 1.3 bcfd, or 59% of offshore Gulf output. However, the impact on overall US supply was limited, as offshore production accounted for just 1.9 bcfd, or 2% of total marketed gas production in 2025. Meanwhile, the latest EIA data showed inventories rose by 85 Bcf in the week ending October 2, exceeding expectations of 79 Bcf and signaling adequate domestic supply. Further weighing on prices, LNG feedgas flows to major US export terminals averaged 17.1 bcfd so far in October, down from 18.0 bcfd in September, largely due to the delayed restart of a liquefaction train at Freeport LNG and planned maintenance at Cove Point. Still, demand for US LNG from European and Asian buyers remained strong amid persistent risks to LNG supplies from the Middle East.

【Supreme People's Court, PBOC, National Financial Regulatory Administration and CSRC publish six model cases on financial consumer and investor protection】Today the Supreme People's Court, the People's Bank of China (PBOC), the National Financial Regulatory Administration and the China Securities Regulatory Commission jointly issued six model cases aimed at protecting financial consumers' and investors' legal rights. The cases cover financial lending, fund and wealth management, digital finance, life insurance and securities investment, and concentrate on: strengthening disclosure obligations for standard-form clauses; enforcing institutional responsibility for suitability management and seller due diligence; removing technical barriers to ensure consumers' choice of remedial procedures; and promoting coordinated governance via multi-track substantive dispute-resolution mechanisms.

Brent Drops on Russian Diesel Supply Hopes — Brent crude fell toward $103 per barrel on Monday, ending a two-session rally as expectations that Russian diesel supplies could help alleviate global fuel shortages weighed on prices. On Friday, President Donald Trump said his administration had reached an agreement for Russia to provide hundreds of thousands of tons of diesel fuel, signaling a partial easing of Moscow’s export ban. Trump also claimed that Russia and Ukraine had agreed to an “energy ceasefire” that would take effect immediately. Meanwhile, Saudi Arabia’s main airport was targeted again on Sunday by Iran-backed Houthi militants, a day after an earlier strike killed 12 people and injured more than 300. The attacks prompted Trump to consider joining Saudi Arabia in retaliatory action against the Houthis in Yemen. Separately, a drone struck a tanker near the Sohar anchorage off Oman over the weekend, just outside the Strait of Hormuz, highlighting persistent risks to global energy supplies.

Silver Gains for Second Session — Silver prices climbed above $61 an ounce on Monday, extending gains from the previous session as falling oil prices, driven by hopes that Russian diesel supplies could ease global fuel shortages, helped alleviate inflation concerns. On Friday, President Donald Trump said his administration had reached an agreement for Russia to supply hundreds of thousands of tons of diesel, signaling a partial lifting of Moscow’s ban on diesel exports. Trump also claimed that Russia and Ukraine had agreed to an “energy ceasefire” effective immediately. Meanwhile, Saudi Arabia’s main airport was targeted again on Sunday by Iran-backed Houthi militants, a day after a strike killed 12 people and injured more than 300. The attacks prompted Trump to consider joining Saudi Arabia in retaliatory action against the Houthis in Yemen. Elsewhere, investors looked ahead to key US inflation figures this week that could offer fresh clues on the Federal Reserve’s next policy move.

### 2026-10-12 09:30:14  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

European Gas Prices Hover Near 4-Week High — European natural gas prices fell below €81/MWh on Monday, having risen to a four-week high earlier as increased tanker attacks in the Strait of Hormuz and escalating regional tensions heightened concerns over the security of LNG supplies. The strait handles around 20% of global LNG trade before the war. Despite a steady rise in European gas inventories in recent weeks, storage facilities are around 73% full, still below year-ago levels. However, this recovery could be threatened by forecasts of cooler autumn temperatures, which may lead to an earlier start to the heating season and increase gas consumption. Last week, European gas network operators said the system was flexible enough to meet winter demand but warned that limited supplies could push storage below 30% by winter’s end, increasing vulnerability to late-season cold snaps. Meanwhile, President Trump said Russia and Ukraine had agreed to an energy ceasefire effective immediately, easing some concerns across the energy market.

South Korean Shares Fall to Over 1-Month Low — The benchmark KOSPI slipped nearly 3% to around 6,440 on Monday, extending losses to an over one-month low as escalating Middle East tensions and elevated oil prices heightened inflation concerns. Renewed Houthi attacks on Saudi Arabia and the possibility of further US involvement raised fears of a broader regional conflict and energy supply disruptions, with Brent crude remaining above USD 104 per barrel. At the same time, elevated US Treasury yields and growing expectations of further monetary tightening weighed on equities. Technology stocks led the decline, with SK hynix and Samsung Electronics falling nearly 4% and more than 2%, respectively, as continued foreign selling overshadowed strong semiconductor earnings and record export growth. Losses were also recorded in SK Square (-3.9%), Doosan Enerbility (-5.1%), Hanwha Aerospace (-2.0%), HD Hyundai Heavy Industries (-3.5%), LG Electronics (-5.0%), and LS Electric (-3.1%).

【美國消費者韌性現裂痕 華爾街警惕美股重要支柱鬆動】儘管物價持續上漲，美國消費者依然不斷花掉自己的工資。柴油價格接近歷史高位之際，他們仍購買皮卡；牛肉價格大漲，他們也照樣享用牛排大餐。這對經濟和市場而言無疑是好訊息，但華爾街擔心這樣的局面難以持久。零售銷售和個人消費支出持續增長，但消費者借貸規模也在同步攀升。與此同時，持續高企的油價和不斷上升的美國國債收益率重創消費者情緒。密歇根大學週五公佈的初步消費者信心指數降至五個月低點，而通脹預期則進一步升高。這一切讓華爾街愈發擔心，這場消費狂歡即將結束，從而抽走支撐美股在歷史高位附近執行的重要支柱。Accuvest Global Advisors首席投資官Eric Clark表示：“消費者不可能永遠承受物價上漲。大多數時候，工資增速趕不上我們所經歷的通脹，這迫使消費者不得不作出取捨。”

【受美以伊戰事影響，超大型油輪運費升至60年來最高水平】近日，美以對伊朗發動的軍事打擊重塑中東原油貿易格局，疊加船隊供給嚴重短缺，目前超大型油輪運費已飆升至60年來最高水平。據荷蘭方面10月11日訊息，荷蘭國際集團最新報告指出，以一艘9月份從沙特出發前往荷蘭的油輪為例，運費折算後相當於每桶原油運輸成本達35美元，超過原油價格的三分之一，而去年同航線每桶原油運輸成本均值僅為2美元。而到了10月，油輪運費依舊高企。據美國方面9日訊息，有船舶經紀商指出，即便經通脹調整，當前運費也已是上世紀60年代超大型油輪問世以來的最高水平。目前，運費暴漲正向整個能源產業鏈傳導。市場的核心問題已經從“運費還能漲多高”轉變為在運費持續攀升侵蝕煉油利潤之前，全球原油貿易體系究竟還能承受多大的壓力。（央視新聞）

Gasoline Prices Decline — US gasoline prices fell toward $3.20 per gallon as traders assessed the prospect of easing supply pressures following President Donald Trump’s claim of an immediate energy ceasefire between Russia and Ukraine. Ukrainian President Volodymyr Zelenskiy said he was awaiting details of the deal, while Russia had yet to comment. Trump’s announcement followed his statement last week that Russia had agreed to supply diesel to global markets to help lower fuel prices, alongside his administration’s temporary easing of US sanctions on Russian fuel. Meanwhile, IEA Executive Director Fatih Birol urged governments to accelerate planned diesel releases and consider boosting supplies. However, renewed strikes on Saudi Arabia’s infrastructure and tanker attacks near the Strait of Hormuz continued to threaten regional energy flows. Elsewhere, Gulf of Mexico oil producers were working to restore offshore output after Hurricane Isaias made landfall.

### 2026-10-12 08:57:19  #公司

【微軟CEO納德拉警告AI模型或構成“內部風險”，呼籲建立獨立管控機制】當地時間10月10日，微軟執行長薩蒂亞·納德拉（Satya Nadella）發文稱，隨著人工智慧模型能力增強，企業正賦予其訪問敏感資料和執行關鍵任務的許可權，但目前仍難以將模型的具體行為和輸出追溯至特定訓練資料或模型引數。他呼籲重新審視AI時代的信任架構，將前沿閉源及開放權重模型視為潛在的“內部風險”，並在模型之外建立獨立的許可權管理與安全控制機制。納德拉提出，企業應確保模型行為可觀測、結果可驗證、許可權可獨立控制，並保留人工隨時暫停或關閉模型的能力。他還強調，應建立模型多樣性、獨立審計和事故及時披露等機制，避免由同一模型同時執行任務並驗證自身結果。

【細化展陳、祭掃、尋親等內容，烈士紀念設施保護新規11月1日起施行】退役軍人事務部訊息，新修訂的《烈士紀念設施保護管理辦法》將於今年11月1日起正式施行。新規補齊零散烈士設施管護短板，規範建設行為，同時細化展陳、祭掃、尋親等內容，讓烈士紀念設施得到更全面規範的保護。新修訂的《烈士紀念設施保護管理辦法》，進一步健全分級保護體系，在完善國家級、省、市、縣級紀念設施常態化管護的基礎上，明確未確定保護級別、零散分佈的烈士紀念設施管護責任，不得擅自改變烈士紀念設施用途，不得買賣、抵押烈士紀念設施。此外，在規劃建設上，新修訂的《烈士紀念設施保護管理辦法》，嚴格規範烈士紀念設施新建、遷建、改擴建行為，嚴控無序建設、重複建設。《辦法》進一步凸顯烈士紀念設施公益屬性，完善涉及文物烈士紀念設施和零散烈士紀念設施的保護管理內容，細化烈士紀念設施改陳布展、祭掃服務有關條款。

### 2026-10-12 08:13:03  #Trading Economics #Economy #Russia #Inflation Rate #Importance 0

Russia Inflation Rate Edges Up in September — Russia’s annual inflation rate edged up to 6.35% in September 2026 from 6.33% in the previous month. Services inflation accelerated to 7.97% from 7.85%, while food price growth eased to 5.05% from 5.08% and non-food inflation slowed to 6.47% from 6.51%. Within food prices, fruit and vegetable prices continued to decline year-on-year, falling 4.24% compared with 5.19% in August, while food products excluding fruits and vegetables rose 5.24% (vs 5.15%). Core inflation increased to 5.47% from 5.37% in the previous month. On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose by 0.35% in September, following a 0.08% decline in August. Average inflation for January–September reached 5.93%, up from 5.87% in the January–August period.

Australia Stocks Extend Gains as Week Begins — Australian shares rose 24 points or 0.3% to 8,740 in early Monday trade, sustaining prior gains as U.S. futures firmed after Wall Street closed higher last week ahead of a busy run of earnings and inflation data. Locally, investors awaited the Reserve Bank's latest meeting minutes later this week, alongside September labor market, business confidence, and leading index figures. Focus in China, Australia's largest trading partner, turned to CPI/PPI and trade performance. However, gains were tempered by geopolitical risks after reports that U.S. President Donald Trump may join Saudi Arabia in retaliating against Iran-backed Houthis in Yemen. Tech, manufacturing, and consumer durables led advances, though steep losses in communications capped momentum. Notable movers included BHP Group (2.3%), Bluescope Steel (3.2%), South32 (2.8%), and Fortescue Ltd. (1.6%), while three of the four major banks added between 0.2% and 0.8%.

【沙特首都機場週日再遭襲擊 而該市即將舉辦重大活動】沙烏地阿拉伯的主要機場週日再次遭到胡塞武裝的襲擊。此前一天，該機場遭到襲擊，造成12人死亡，給這個中東最大經濟體帶來巨大壓力，因為該國正準備舉辦旨在促進外國投資的大型活動。沙特領導的聯軍在一份宣告中表示，週日一枚不明射彈擊中了利雅得的哈立德國王國際機場的一座客運航站樓，當局正在撲滅火災。聯軍正在葉門與伊朗支援的胡塞武裝作戰。胡塞武裝迅速宣稱對此次襲擊負責，稱這是對“沙特侵略”的回應，其中包括對葉門首都薩那機場的襲擊。沙特民航局稱，此次襲擊造成1人死亡，11人受傷。聯軍方面也表示，防空部隊在沙特首都同一地點附近攔截了一枚導彈。此前在哈立德國王國際機場週六遭到的襲擊中，還有300多人受傷，這是自2月伊朗戰爭爆發以來沙烏地阿拉伯境內傷亡最慘重的單次事件。另外，沙特民航局在社交媒體平臺X上發帖稱，達曼的法赫德國王國際機場週日晚間遭到襲擊。當局已表示，原定於週日至10月15日舉行的第25屆世界石油大會及相關活動將如期舉行。此外，利雅得還計劃於本月晚些時候舉辦未來投資倡議活動，這是利雅得最重要的投資者盛會。

Brent Edges Higher on Saudi Airport Attack — Brent crude prices rose toward $105 per barrel on Monday, marking a third consecutive session of gains as Houthi attacks on Saudi Arabia’s main airport raised fears of a wider conflict that could disrupt energy flows further. Riyadh’s King Khalid International Airport was targeted again on Sunday by Iran-backed Houthi militants, a day after an earlier strike killed 12 people and injured more than 300, making it Saudi Arabia’s deadliest single incident since the Iran war began. Meanwhile, a drone struck a tanker near the Sohar anchorage off Oman over the weekend, just outside the Strait of Hormuz, underscoring still-elevated supply risks. Separately, US President Donald Trump said Russia and Ukraine had agreed to an “energy ceasefire” that would take effect immediately. In the US, the Department of Energy announced approval of an emergency exchange involving up to 4 million barrels of crude oil from strategic reserves to help mitigate supply disruptions caused by Hurricane Isaias.

【7月以來均衡基金超八成下跌 首尾業績相差近54個百分點】近期，伴隨市場波動加劇，主動權益基金新發產品中，均衡類產品成為公募佈局的重要方向。比如，平安均衡精選、中歐均衡增長等產品均定於10月開啟認購。資料顯示，截至2026年10月9日，年內成立的均衡主動權益基金（以名稱中帶“均衡”的基金為統計口徑）數量達14只，年度成立數量創2022年以來新高。均衡類產品的設計初衷，是依靠更廣域的選股佈局，透過多行業分散配置來平滑組合波動，減少單一板塊劇烈波動給淨值帶來的衝擊，儘可能改善持有人的持有體驗。不過，從已成立的均衡基金來看，7月以來業績表現分化明顯，下跌產品多達69只，佔比超85%，首尾業績相差近54個百分點。短期不抗跌，究竟是策略失效，還是風格極致分化下的暫時失色？據證券時報基金研究院統計，截至10月9日，在50餘隻成立滿3年的均衡基金中，85.5%的基金近3年取得正收益，這一比例高於主動權益基金81.2%的整體水平。其中華商均衡成長A等20只產品收益率超31.5%，跑贏同期萬得偏股混合型基金指數。

【7月以來均衡基金超八成下跌 首尾業績相差近54個百分點】近期，伴隨市場波動加劇，主動權益基金新發產品中，均衡類產品成為公募佈局的重要方向。比如，平安均衡精選、中歐均衡增長等產品均定於10月開啟認購。資料顯示，截至2026年10月9日，年內成立的均衡主動權益基金（以名稱中帶“均衡”的基金為統計口徑）數量達14只，年度成立數量創2022年以來新高。均衡類產品的設計初衷，是依靠更廣域的選股佈局，透過多行業分散配置來平滑組合波動，減少單一板塊劇烈波動給淨值帶來的衝擊，儘可能改善持有人的持有體驗。不過，從已成立的均衡基金來看，7月以來業績表現分化明顯，首尾業績相差近54個百分點。短期不抗跌，究竟是策略失效，還是風格極致分化下的暫時失色？據證券時報基金研究院統計，截至10月9日，在50餘隻成立滿3年的均衡基金中，85.5%的基金近3年取得正收益，這一比例高於主動權益基金81.2%的整體水平。其中華商均衡成長A等20只產品收益率超31.5%，跑贏同期萬得偏股混合型基金指數。

### 2026-10-12 07:09:56  #Trading Economics #Economy #Senegal #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Senegal Inflation Rate Eases to 1% in September — Senegal’s annual inflation rate slowed to 1.0% in September 2026 from August’s three-month high of 1.2%. The deceleration came as food and beverages inflation, the largest component of the inflation basket, eased to 0.9% from a four-month high of 1.4% in August. Inflation also moderated for transport (1.2% vs 1.7% in August), alcoholic beverages and tobacco (12.0% vs 12.6%), and financial services (1.0% vs 1.1%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 1.2%, slowing from a 2.0% gain in the prior month, which was the highest level since July 2023.

### 2026-10-11 22:57:55  #公司 #央行 #國際

【美聯儲傳聲筒Nick Timiraos釋出文章：美聯儲官員哈馬克傾向於採取“幅度更小、頻率更高”的加息策略，以避免未來被迫進行更大幅度的加息】在克利夫蘭的工業硬體製造商Jergens公司，貝絲·哈馬克（Beth Hammack）瞭解到，在客戶的優先考量清單上，價格已降至第三位，排在質量和交付速度之後。誠然，加息既無法打通霍爾木茲海峽，也無法立即給資料中心的建設熱潮降溫。但加息或許能讓其他企業在當前環境下對擴張計劃“三思而後行”，從而將緊缺的零部件或供應留給資料中心領域，並在邊際上緩解供需壓力。她傾向於採取“幅度更小、頻率更高”的加息策略，以避免未來被迫進行更大幅度的加息。她對中性利率的估算在美聯儲官員中位居前列，而她表示，如果自己的估算是錯的，那也只可能是估低了。此外，她強調，收益率上升“絕對無法”替代美聯儲的加息行動。

【2026中國網球公開賽多項資料創新高】中國網球公開賽組委會11日召開新聞釋出會，通報今年賽事的各項資料。其中，比賽場次、觀眾人數、商業運營資料等多項資料再創新高。賽事期間，國家網球中心入園總人數預計超45萬人，同比增長25%；其中京外觀眾佔比60%，境外觀眾近萬人。賽事連續6天單日入園人數超過3.5萬，10月1日、2日均突破4萬人。國慶期間，中網賽事熱度也轉化為城市消費活力。截至目前，中網整體票房達1.32億元，同比增長48%，創歷史新高；賽期綜合消費流水額預計超5500萬元，同比增長55%；特許商品銷售流水額2800萬元，較去年翻番。根據北京市體育局“雙奧”100賽事評估資料，本屆賽事產生直接經濟影響25.90億元，間接經濟影響66.71億元。（新華社）

### 2026-10-11 20:06:21  #其他

【不看景點看工廠，“洋廠長”扎堆來華取經，專看三大賽道】據公開報道，近日，有一個正快速升溫的現象：越來越多的海外投資人、企業高管、學界精英、青年創業者正組團，專程來中國考察。他們集中考察北京、上海、深圳、杭州、合肥等地的新能源汽車、鋰電池、人形機器人、人工智慧等行業企業。三大創新線路最受德企青睞。“參觀考察旅行通常持續約10天、訪問4個城市，行程很緊湊。”Andrea Höcker（何雪花）所在的中國國際投資促進中心（德國）主要為德語區（即DACH地區：德國、奧地利、瑞士）提供雙向投資促進服務，來自德國的中小企業決策者、地方政府代表、產業叢集代表，一年來三次中國，考察這裡的汽車、智慧製造、生命科學產業。“過去，中國企業前往德國（的目的是）學習，並且主要是（學習）傳統產業。”何雪花提到，現在企業家們都帶著明確目標前往中國，考察主題也已轉移：從傳統產業轉向智慧製造、人工智慧、機器人技術和生命科學。她告訴記者，與汽車、智慧製造（特別是機械工程、機器人和人工智慧）以及生命科學（特別是生物技術、製藥和醫療器械）三大產業相匹配的目的地、路線，受到了德國中小企業的決策者、地方政府代表、產業叢集代表等參與者的廣泛歡迎。

---

【CITIC Securities: Favor cyclical and supply-rationalization trades; monitor AI investment-cycle inflection】CITIC Securities says oil stabilisation, softer-than-expected US nonfarm payrolls and downgrades to Fed rate-hike odds have not reversed the rise in global long-term yields. The firm attributes higher yields to persistently strong private-sector investment and financing demand—trillions in investment have driven North America out of the post-crisis low-growth, low-rate norm. A sustained high-rate global environment is likely until an inflection in the AI investment cycle. Demand relatively insensitive to elevated overseas rates is concentrated in North American AI and sectors tied to expanded Chinese central fiscal spending. Former outperformers such as offshore exporters and resource plays face pressure. In weak demand conditions, assets benefiting from supply rationalization are scarce; the unwinding of excessive domestic competition next year merits attention. Tactical positioning: in the near term use cyclical, high-momentum sectors and supply-rationalization beneficiaries to cope with high rates; closely monitor the AI investment-cycle turning point.

【CITIC Securities: US earnings boom is broadening beyond top tech; non-tech contributions rising】CITIC Securities says investor focus will shift to US Q3 earnings after US equities showed some rate-decoupling over the domestic holiday. LSEG consensus shows S&P 500 Q3 2026 revenue and EPS YoY growth of 1.2% and 45.0%, respectively, with QoQ moderation. At the industry level, energy, information technology, materials and healthcare all report YoY earnings growth above 50% — indicating high earnings growth is no longer confined to leading tech names and that non-tech contributions are rising materially. For Hong Kong stocks, higher global rates plus renewed AI trading have kept liquidity under pressure, but fundamentals appear to have bottomed and earnings-growth forecasts for major broad indices are being revised up. Earnings revisions are highly divergent by sector: some niche segments are being upgraded while domestic-demand exposed sectors still face downward revisions. Q3 results will be the key test of any recovery. CITIC recommends patient positioning in Hong Kong equities and expects short-term dividend-focused strategies to relatively outperform.

【Anthropic AI model submitted false tip to Philadelphia police】Multiple U.S. media reported on Oct. 10 that in July ANTHROPIC’s AI model submitted a false unsolved-homicide tip to the Philadelphia Police Department’s public web form. 【Jefferies基金稱Radiant World在價值9億美元融資交易中存在欺詐】Jefferies旗下一支基金在法庭訴訟中表示，它掌握了鐵礦石貿易商Radiant World和Sapphire Minmetals在面值近9億美元的應收賬款融資交易中涉嫌欺詐行為的證據。這一說法由該基金的律師在一份書面陳述中提出，大大擴充套件了人們對這起涉嫌欺詐案規模的瞭解。彭博新聞社此前曾報道，該案可能成為自安然公司以來大宗商品行業最大的欺詐案之一。在此之前，Radiant World公司約5億美元的發票，已由其他銀行和債權人，在另外的法律訴訟中指控存在欺詐行為，這包括瑞穗金融集團、德意志銀行、義大利聯合聖保羅銀行和Incomlend Pte。Jefferies基金的律師Christopher Bushell在書面陳述中稱，這些公司“大規模地”偽造和篡改法律和財務檔案。律師還指控，Radiant World和Sapphire Minmetals公司屢次試圖阻止該基金聯絡其所謂的客戶，以確認其用以融資的應收賬款真實性。Radiant World和Sapphire Minmetals同屬一個法律上獨立但聯絡緊密的公司集團。這些公司一再否認存在不當行為。

【百合花：董事王迪明擬減持不超0.1404%公司股份】百合花集團股份有限公司於2026年10月12日披露董事減持計劃公告。截至公告日，公司董事王迪明持有公司無限售流通股份2,337,872股，佔公司總股本的0.5615%，股份來源於IPO前取得及資本公積轉增，其同時持有控股股東百合花控股10%的股權，無一致行動人。王迪明因自身資金需求，計劃自公告發布之日起15個交易日之後的3個月內（即2026年11月3日至2027年2月2日），透過集中競價方式減持不超過584,400股，佔其持股總數的25%，約佔公司總股本的0.1404%，減持價格依據市場價格確定。若公告披露期間公司股票發生停牌，實際減持起始時間將相應順延。王迪明已作出IPO鎖定期、董監高任職期間減持比例、減持價格、鎖定期延長等相關承諾，本次減持事項與此前披露的承諾一致。本次減持計劃實施存在減持時間、價格、數量等不確定性，不會對公司治理結構及持續經營產生重大影響，亦不會導致公司控制權發生變更，減持符合相關法律法規要求，減持實施期間將嚴格履行資訊披露義務。

【China corporate credit index edges up in August】China’s corporate credit index rose to 161.32 in August, up 2.45 points MoM, the State Administration for Market Regulation said. Operational and regulatory subindices drove the improvement; new entries to the abnormal-operations list fell sharply while removals increased and the share of high-credit-risk firms declined, supporting a stabilization and rebound in overall credit. Top five sectors by index were financials, manufacturing, water/environment and public facilities management, utilities (power/heat/gas/water), and transportation/warehousing/postal; manufacturing, transport/warehousing/postal and wholesale & retail posted the fastest MoM gains, with manufacturing at its highest rank this year.

### 2026-10-11 14:15:17  #Trading Economics #Economy #Saudi Arabia #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Saudi Arabia Industrial Output Slumps 23.5% in August — Industrial production in Saudi Arabia plunged 23.5% year-on-year in August 2026, following an upwardly revised 8.3% drop in the previous month. The oil sector slumped 33.66% after an 11.4% drop in July, largely reflecting steeper declines in both the extraction of crude petroleum and natural gas (-35.8% vs. -10.9%) and the output of coke and refined petroleum products (-24.1% vs. -13.6%). However, the decline was partly offset by faster growth in output in other economic activities (5.1% vs 3.4%), with electricity, gas, steam, and air-conditioning supply growing much faster than in July (9.4% vs 4.5%), while water supply, sewerage, waste management, and remediation activities rose more slowly (7.4% vs. 7.8%). The decline was also offset by a rebound in output in non-oil activities (1.2% vs -0.3%), though the manufacturing sector edged down 0.3%. On a monthly basis, industrial output fell 14.0% in August, reversing sharply from a downwardly revised 10.0% increase in July.

據悉，靈光2.0的內測用例集中在長程、變數多、普通人難盯住的場景，例如幫上班族跟蹤市場異動、幫家長管理釘釘打卡、旅行中遇航班延誤或景區限流重排路線等。（新浪科技）

【上海鏈家“誠意賣”成交破100單，平均成交週期縮短至8天】10月11日，上海鏈家披露，自9月16號上新至今三週，全新服務“誠意賣”成交突破100單。成交房源中，近四分之一此前掛牌超過90天，平均掛牌天數達63天；而透過“誠意賣”成交後，平均成交週期縮短至8天左右。而中指研究院資料顯示，2025年上海全市二手房平均成交週期約為102天。“誠意賣”將二手房交易中最耗時的談價環節進行標準化改造：業主設定起始價、可談價和心理底價三個價格層級；買賣方各繳納5萬元保證金至第三方監管賬戶；買家最多出價兩次，達到底價即自動鎖定成交。該服務在置換、異地賣房型兩類需求上表現突出。一位掛牌超一年的置換業主，進入“誠意賣”後4天成交；而異地業主則可全流程線上操作完成簽約。（澎湃）

【Australian home auction clearance rate below 50% for second week】Australia's home auction clearance rate slipped below 50% for a second straight week after the Reserve Bank of Australia raised rates to a 15-year high, weighing on buyer demand. Cotality data show 1,781 properties went to auction across six state capitals last week, with a 48.8% clearance rate versus 45.4% the prior week. National house prices in September fell back to year-ago levels; higher borrowing costs and May budget tax changes that limit investor concessions have damped demand. Sydney prices are down nearly 9% from the February peak. Auctions, common in Sydney and Melbourne, normally see lower clearance rates in downturns as seller price expectations diverge from market levels.

【為何接陌生來電要先沉默三秒？國家安全部提醒：這類隱私侵害在升級】國家安全部微信公眾號發文，近期，一條“接到陌生來電先沉默三秒”的提醒在社交平臺刷屏。該提醒指出，不法分子在接通受害者電話後，可能刻意保持沉默，誘使受害者發出“喂”或“哪位”等回應。短短數秒的語音樣本，就可能被不法分子用於AI聲紋克隆，進而冒充身份實施詐騙等違法犯罪行為。因此，接聽電話可先沉默三秒，若對方也持續沉默，就要及時結束通話。這一提醒為我們敲響了生物資訊保安的警鐘。

【農業農村部與山西省政府舉行工作會談】農業農村部10月10日與山西省政府舉行工作會談。農業農村部黨組書記、部長張柱，山西省委副書記、省長盧東亮出席會談。張柱表示，山西省委省政府堅持把“三農”工作擺在重中之重位置，大力推進糧食生產、鞏固拓展脫貧攻堅成果、旱作農業發展、鄉村特色產業培育和鄉村建設等工作，取得一系列明顯成效。農業農村部將認真貫徹落實習近平總書記關於“三農”工作的重要論述和對山西工作的重要講話重要指示精神，大力支援山西抓好糧食生產，紮實推進高標準農田建設，高質量發展旱作農業，整合推廣水肥一體化等綜合性技術模式，促進大面積均衡增產，提升農業綜合生產能力和質量效益。做優做精鄉村富民產業，推進農產品精深加工，完善聯農帶農機制，促進農民群眾就業增收。落實好常態化幫扶政策要求，守牢不發生規模性返貧致貧底線，進一步增強內生動力和自我發展能力。深入學習運用“千萬工程”經驗，因地制宜建設宜居宜業和美鄉村，合理補齊農村現代生活條件短板。希望部省加強聯動、同頻共振，合力加快山西農業農村現代化。盧東亮代表省委、省政府感謝農業農村部的關心支援，表示將深入貫徹習近平總書記關於“三農”工作的重要論述和對山西工作的重要講話重要指示精神，聚焦打造特優農業和有機旱作基地，堅決扛牢保障糧食安全政治責任，堅持穩面積、增單產兩手抓，分割槽分類推進高標準農田建設，抓實抓細常態化精準幫扶工作，深入實施特優農業發展戰略，紮實推進農業科技創新，大力培育農業經營主體，加強農產品質量安全監管，有力有效推進鄉村全面振興。希望農業農村部加強政策指導，支援山西持續鞏固拓展脫貧攻堅成果，開展旱地高標準農田改造建設，建強農業科技創新平臺，推動特優農業提質增效，高質量舉辦鄉村振興（太谷）論壇，助力山西農業農村現代化邁上新臺階。農業農村部黨組成員、副部長張興旺，山西省政府副省長常書銘出席會談。

【禮來公佈依奇珠單抗與替爾泊肽合併用藥臨床研究最新資料】10月10日，禮來公司公佈了TOGETHER-PsO3b期臨床研究的最新探索性資料。該研究旨在評估對於患有中重度斑塊狀銀屑病合併肥胖或超重且至少伴有一種其他體重相關合併症的成人患者，依奇珠單抗與替爾泊肽合併用藥相較於依奇珠單抗單藥治療的療效和安全性。資料顯示，與單獨使用依奇珠單抗相比，依奇珠單抗與替爾泊肽合併用藥治療後，更多與銀屑病疾病及代謝相關的生物標誌物發生了變化。

【AI buying fades in South Korea; turnover down 70% from late-May as foreigners exit】South Korea’s equity market has seen AI-driven buying evaporate: market turnover is down about 70% from a late‑May peak as foreign investors accelerate withdrawals and domestic retail selling intensifies. The reversal reflects heavy concentration in Samsung Electronics and SK Hynix. Phillip Wool, portfolio manager at Rayliant Global Advisors, said his fund has been taking profits on Korean AI names and is underweight SK Hynix and Samsung. Julius Baer’s Richard Tang said capital is rotating back into US equities, prompting sustained foreign outflows from Korean stocks. UBS Global Wealth Management’s Chun‑Lai Wu said the firm prefers Taiwan equities for AI exposure, citing a more complete hardware ecosystem and stronger capex plans among major tech companies.

【三季度高頻資料折射我國經濟向好態勢】記者11日從國家發展改革委國家資訊中心瞭解到，三季度，消費、外貿、投資等領域多項高頻資料平穩向好，我國經濟頂壓前行，保持穩中有進、穩中向優態勢，增長動能不斷積蓄。 線下消費大資料顯示，三季度，各類消費支付金額同比增長2.6%，增速較二季度提高0.7個百分點。9月線下支付金額同比增長3.1%，增速較8月提高0.5個百分點，連續3個月上行。 “三季度以來，我國各項促消費政策持續擴圍加力，居民消費意願穩步回暖，市場消費需求不斷釋放，超大規模市場優勢進一步凸顯，為擴內需、穩增長提供了有力支撐。”國家發展改革委國家資訊中心大資料發展部副主任魏穎說。 資料顯示，三季度主要港口貨運船舶離港、到港載重同比分別增長43.1%、0.3%，較二季度提高43.0、0.7個百分點。其中，9月主要港口貨運船舶離港、到港載重同比分別增長53.2%、4.5%。面對複雜嚴峻的國際環境，我國外貿繼續保持高景氣態勢，展現出強大韌性和活力，為全球市場注入更多確定性。（新華社）

【四川省“專精特新”專板企業賦能券正式推出 新入板企業年度申領額度八萬元】10月10日，記者從省經濟和資訊化廳獲悉，省經濟和資訊化廳、省財政廳近日聯合印發《四川省“專精特新”專板企業賦能券實施方案》（下稱《方案》），正式推出四川省“專精特新”專板企業賦能券。 該賦能券是支援企業在進入四川省區域性股權市場“專精特新”專板（下稱四川省“專精特新”專板）規範培育中購買服務的一種普惠性政策工具。簡單來講，它是一張可抵扣專板企業服務費用的電子版“代金券”，採用電子券形式直接面向專板企業發放，全省通用通兌，每年度實行總額管控，先申先享、發完即止。企業接受納入四川省“專精特新”專板服務機構庫的機構提供的規定範圍內服務後，可按約定使用賦能券抵扣服務費用，再由服務機構按程式申請資金兌付。 《方案》明確，每戶企業每年可申領一次賦能券，其中，當年新入板企業年度申領額度8萬元，以前年度已入板企業年度申領額度5萬元。賦能券發放後當年（自然年）有效，每年統一兌付；僅限專板企業申領使用，不得轉讓、贈送、買賣、質押、出借。（四川日報）

### 2026-10-11 08:22:02  #公司

【微軟CEO稱AI是潛在內部威脅】微軟公司CEO薩提亞·納德拉(Satya Nadella)表示，企業應將強大的AI模型視為潛在的內部威脅，假定它們可能已被攻破，並建立一套“緊急剎車”系統，以防止智慧體模型失控。 納德拉週六在X平臺上發帖稱，部署先進AI的企業不應僅僅依賴AI模型開發商的安全保證。 “我們必須假定模型已被攻破，並從一開始就對其加以限制。可以把它想象成緊急剎車。獲得授權的人員應當始終能夠在模型執行任務的過程中暫停或關閉它。”納德拉表示。 就在納德拉發表上述言論之際，Anthropic和OpenAI近幾個月披露了一系列AI模型出現非預期行為的事件，包括Anthropic的一款模型在一起警方調查的謀殺案中提交虛假線索，以及AI模型多次入侵第三方網站。這些披露加劇了外界對前沿AI安全風險的擔憂，也讓所謂的AI“緊急關閉開關”再次成為討論焦點。

【四川：力爭5年新增規上工業企業6000戶】10月10日，記者從省經濟和資訊化廳獲悉，近日印發的《四川省促進中小企業發展工作方案（2026—2030年）》提出，到2030年，力爭全省5年累計新增規上工業企業6000戶，創新型中小企業達2萬戶，專精特新中小企業達7000戶，專精特新“小巨人”企業達700戶，國家級中小企業特色產業叢集達40個。 《方案》從八個方面提出具體任務。培育壯大經營主體方面，要推動小微企業上規提質，強化小微企業穩規幫扶，將實施小微企業上規提質專項行動。支援專精特新發展方面，要加強優質企業梯度培育，探索重點省級專精特新中小企業躍級發展，加強重點“小巨人”申報推薦和跟蹤服務。支援產業創新發展方面，要強化中小企業科技創新能力，創新科技攻關供需對接機制，將實施中小企業科技創新能力提升行動，構建“三無研發”重點規上工業企業清零攻堅機制。支援數智綠色發展方面，將實施中小企業數字化賦能專項行動，支援中小企業特色產業叢集完善綠色低碳公共設施，培育綠色園區和零碳園區等。支援聚鏈成群發展方面，要促進中小企業叢集化發展，推動大中小企業融通發展，提升穩鏈強鏈能力。《方案》還對加大金融支援力度、提升對企服務水平、強化營商環境建設等方面進行專門部署。保障措施上，將明確健全跨部門協調和省市縣三級聯動機制，統籌用好各級財政資金，加強政策宣傳解讀和典型經驗推廣，營造有利於中小企業高質量發展的良好氛圍。（四川日報）

【US DOJ probes major networks' pooled White House interview-sharing model】The US Justice Department is investigating television networks' pooled White House interview‑sharing arrangement, sources said. Targets include ABC, CBS, CNN, NBC and Fox News. DOJ officials contacted the networks on Friday and requested communications and materials related to the White House pool‑sharing mechanism, under which networks take turns filming presidential events and distribute footage to other outlets. Last month, after Trump barred a CNN reporter from White House grounds, the networks suspended pooled coverage for several days, excluding CNN. The probe signals an escalation in the ongoing conflict between the Trump administration and the White House press corps.

【美軍癱瘓一艘試圖違反封鎖令的貨船】美國中央司令部（CENTCOM）表示，10月10日，美軍在阿曼灣癱瘓了一艘商用貨船。此前，該船船員無視多次警告，並試圖突破美國針對伊朗持續實施的海上封鎖。一架美軍戰鬥機使用精確制導武器擊中了懸掛巴拿馬國旗的“海洋莫利卡”號貨船（M/V Ocean Molica，又名“Arika Sun”）的船尾，使該船推進系統完全癱瘓，但未造成船員傷亡。這艘大型貨船此前曾駛離伊朗港口，事發時正試圖穿越該地區水域。美軍於7月14日恢復了對所有進出伊朗港口船隻的海上封鎖。在為期三個月的嚴格執法期間，已有4艘商船被癱瘓，135艘船隻被攔截並改變航向。此外，在同一時期，美軍還摧毀了10艘與伊斯蘭革命衛隊數十億美元“影子船隊”網路有關的油輪。美國中央司令部稱，建議所有航海人員注意並聽從美軍中央司令部的封鎖警告。在阿曼灣和霍爾木茲海峽附近水域航行時，船員應繼續留意航海通告廣播，並透過甚高頻16頻道與美國海軍聯絡。

### 2026-10-11 08:07:03  #公司 #市場 #國際

【儲存晶片紅利消退 千億美元外資加速撤離韓股】韓國今年很長一段時間都是全球火熱人工智慧交易行情的標杆。而如今，無論從哪項指標來看，這個總規模4.3萬億美元的股票市場正在快速失去投資者的關注。 市場成交額較5月末峰值暴跌70%，外資正以亞洲最快速度撤離，本土散戶投資者也在退出。韓國綜合股價指數（Kospi）上半年曾是全球表現最好的主要股指，此後累計下跌22%，成為下半年表現最差的主要股指；而同樣重倉AI板塊的美國股市股指則不斷創新高。 行情反轉背後，韓國股市的AI紅利高度依賴三星電子與SK海力士這兩大儲存晶片巨頭，二者處於全球AI供應鏈的核心位置。這種高度集中的特性如今成為短板：投資者開始質疑儲存晶片繁榮週期能否持續，疊加今夏由高槓杆引發的劇烈拋售，不少全球基金對重返韓國市場持謹慎態度。 這兩家晶片企業合計佔韓國綜合股價指數權重一半以上，是今年早些時候拉動指數上漲的主力。交易所資料顯示，今年外資從韓國股市撤出1310億美元，在亞洲主要市場中規模最高。

### 2026-10-11 07:27:16  #宏觀

【食品安全法大幅修訂回應哪些關切？專家解讀】10月10日，市場監管總局就《中華人民共和國食品安全法（修訂草案徵求意見稿）》（以下簡稱《徵求意見稿》）面向社會公開徵求意見。這是食品安全法自2009年頒佈施行、2015年較大幅度修訂之後的一次大規模系統性修訂。

校園食品安全供餐體量大、用餐群體敏感、社會關注程度高。《徵求意見稿》針對“學校食品安全”增設專節，確立校長（園長）負責制，建立中小學、幼兒園集中用餐陪餐制度。

“本次修訂實現了從平臺責任到平臺內經營者、從傳統電商到直播營銷的全覆蓋。”中國人民公安大學教授李春雷介紹，比如將責任主體從“網路食品交易第三方平臺提供者”統一規範為“網路食品交易平臺經營者”，將管理物件從“入網食品經營者”擴充套件為“平臺內經營者、直播間運營者”，首次將直播帶貨等新業態納入調整範圍。（人民日報）

【知情人士：美政府正調查電視網涉特朗普“共享報道”一事】據六名知情人士透露，美國司法部正在調查美國主要電視網——ABC、CBS、CNN、NBC和福克斯新聞——在集體暫停對特朗普的“共享報道”時，是否違反了聯邦反壟斷法。這些知情人士稱，司法部官員已於週五與各大電視網取得聯絡，要求他們提供與白宮電視共享報道機制相關的通訊記錄和材料。該機制是一項長期存在的制度，由各大電視網輪流負責拍攝總統活動，並將影片素材分發給其他新聞機構。上個月，在總統禁止CNN記者進入白宮園區後，各大電視網集體暫停了對特朗普的共享報道長達數天，這導致CNN無法參與該共享報道機制。 司法部的這項調查標誌著特朗普政府與白宮記者團之間持續衝突的嚴重升級。

【需求持續回暖 9月中國物流業景氣指數釋出】中國物流與採購聯合會今天（11日）釋出9月份中國物流業景氣指數。指數繼續保持在擴張區間，較上月有所回升，物流需求持續回暖，企業經營韌性不斷顯現。9月份，中國物流業景氣指數為51.5%，較上月回升0.6個百分點，連續5個月處在擴張區間。新訂單指數為51.2%，環比回升0.4個百分點，連續7個月回升。12個分項指數中，有10個分項指數環比回升。從三季度以來的資料變化看，物流業務需求增長，微觀經營趨穩，為四季度物流景氣平穩執行奠定基礎。（央視新聞）
